## Canada Grain Regulations {#canada-grain-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[C.R.C.]{.abbr title="Consolidated Regulations of Canada"}, c. 889
:::

[CANADA GRAIN ACT](/eng/acts/G-10)

Canada Grain Regulations
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in these Regulations.

[[accountable gross weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[accountable gross weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2004-198, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Canada Grain
    Act](/eng/acts/G-10). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[grading of unofficial samples]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[grading of unofficial samples]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2001-273, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[gross handling variance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[gross handling variance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2013-111, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[gross handling variance percentage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[gross handling variance
    percentage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2013-111, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[hazardous substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hazardous substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any pesticide,
    desiccant or inoculant. ([substance dangereuse]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[inland terminal elevator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[inland terminal elevator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2001-273, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[net weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[net weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the gross weight of grain
    less the amount of dockage specified on the elevator or grain
    receipt or cash purchase ticket issued in respect of the
    grain. ([poids net]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[regional inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[regional inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2004-198, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote

- SOR/2001-273, s. 1

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Canadian Grain Commission]{.HTitleText1} {#h-581582 .Part}

### [Fees of the Commission]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581583 .Subheading}

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** The fees of the Commission
for services performed under the Act are set out in Schedule 1.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-55, s. 1
- SOR/88-408, s. 1
- SOR/89-376, s. 14(F)
- SOR/95-386, s. 1
- SOR/96-508, s. 1
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2001-273, s. 2(E)
- SOR/2003-284, s. 1
- SOR/2004-198, s. 2
:::

**[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** On April 1, 2019 and
every subsequent year on April 1, all fees set out in Schedule 1 will be
adjusted by the percentage change over 12 months in the April All-items
Consumer Price Index for Canada, as published by Statistics Canada under
the [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19), for the previous fiscal year. All
adjusted fees are calculated to the nearest cent.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-33, s. 1
:::

### [Oath or Solemn Affirmation of Commissioner]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581590 .Subheading}

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** A commissioner's oath or
solemn affirmation of office shall be taken as it appears in Form 1 of
Schedule 2.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-55, s. 2
- SOR/81-610, s. 1
- SOR/96-508, s. 2
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2001-273, s. 3
- SOR/2003-284, s. 2
:::

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Grain Grades, Grading and Inspection]{.HTitleText1} {#h-581594 .Part}

### [Oath or Solemn Affirmation of Member of Grain Standards Committee]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581595 .Subheading}

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** The oath or solemn
affirmation of office of a member of a grain standards committee who is
not a commissioner and who is not employed in the public service of
Canada shall be taken as it appears in Form 2 of Schedule 2.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-508, s. 3
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2001-273, s. 4
- SOR/2003-284, s. 3

SOR/2013-111, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

[Previous
Version](section-4.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 4.1"){.PITLink}

### [Grain Grades]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581600 .Subheading}

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following seeds are designated as grain for the purposes of the Act:
  barley, beans, buckwheat, canary seed, canola, chickpeas, corn, faba
  beans, flaxseed, lentils, mixed grain, mustard seed, oats, peas,
  rapeseed, rye, safflower seed, soybeans, sunflower seed, triticale and
  wheat.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The grade names and the specifications for grades of
  grain are those set out in Schedule 3.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-508, s. 4
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2003-285, s. 1
- SOR/2013-111, s. 3

  role="button"}

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1447987-1448405.html"
  role="button"}

Version](section-5-20210801.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}

### [Official Samples]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581606 .Subheading}

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  official sample taken under section 30 of the Act shall be taken in
  accordance with Chapter 2 of the Commission's Sampling Systems
  Handbook and Approval Guide, as amended from time to time.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No official samples shall be taken by means of a
  mechanical sampler unless it is installed, tested and maintained by
  the licensee under the direction of an inspector.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An official sample shall be retained for a period
  beginning on the day on which the sample is graded and ending not less
  than six months, but not more than 120 months, after that day.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-813, s. 1
- SOR/89-395, s. 2(F)

- SOR/2001-273, s. 5

- SOR/2015-137, s. 1

### [Official Inspection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581616 .Subheading}

**[[6.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.1 .sectionLabel}** When a licensed grain
dealer or the operator of a licensed elevator requests an official
inspection of grain, they shall advise the Commission of the origin of
the grain, or of the fact that the grain is of mixed origin, on the
appropriate form supplied by the Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-217, s. 1
:::

### [Terminal Elevator and Third Party Samples]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581620 .Subheading}

**[[6.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.2 .sectionLabel}** A sample taken as part
of an inspection under subsection 70(1), (2) or 70.2(1) or section 70.3
of the Act shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be taken in accordance with Chapter 2 of the
  Commission's Sampling Systems Handbook and Approval Guide, as amended
  from time to time; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} be retained for not less than seven days after the
  date of the grading of the sample.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-111, s. 5
- SOR/2015-137, s. 2
- [SOR/2024-43, s. 3](#1447994-1448410){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1447994-1448410.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6.2-20150801.html "Link to previous version of section 6.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Grading of Unofficial Samples]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581626 .Subheading}

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  licensee or any person not required to be licensed or exempted from
  licensing under section 44 of the Act may forward an unofficial sample
  of grain to any regional inspection office of the Commission for a
  determination of one or more of its grade, dockage and other quality
  factors.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A sample forwarded under subsection (1) shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consist of at least 1 kg;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be taken in such a manner as to ensure that it is
    an average and representative sample of the parcel of grain from
    which it is taken; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be forwarded, with all shipping charges prepaid, in
    a container that will maintain the integrity of the sample.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The shipper of a sample forwarded to a regional
  inspection office under this section shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} state on a form supplied by the Commission that
    accompanies the sample the name and post office address of each
    person to whom the report as to the grade, dockage and other quality
    factors of the sample is to be sent; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} place on the form a distinguishing number or mark
    of identification that the shipper has not previously used in
    respect of any other sample during that crop year.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of a sample and its accompanying form
  forwarded in accordance with this section, an inspector shall examine
  the sample and make the requested determination of its grade, dockage
  or other quality factors and transmit a copy of the determination in
  writing to each person named on the form.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Any person who has an interest in the grain and who
  is dissatisfied with the determination of the inspector may, within 15
  days after the date of the examination, request the chief grain
  inspector for Canada to re-examine the sample.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the sample, the chief grain inspector
  for Canada shall examine it, determine its grade, dockage and other
  quality factors and transmit a copy of the determination to each
  person named in the request.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The determination of the chief grain inspector for
  Canada is final.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} An unofficial sample shall be retained for the period
  beginning on the date of the grading of the sample and ending not less
  than 20 days after that date.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 11(F), 14(F), 16(F)
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2003-284, s. 6
- SOR/2004-198, s. 4
- SOR/2005-361, s. 2
- SOR/2013-111, s. 6
:::

Version](section-7-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Disposal of Samples]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581643 .Subheading}

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Official
  samples and unofficial samples forwarded to the Commission for grading
  shall be sold by public tender within a reasonable time after the end
  of the required retention period for the samples under subsection 6(3)
  or 7(8).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2005-361, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If no tender is received by the Commission for the
  purchase of an official sample or unofficial sample, the sample shall
  be discarded.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 1, 12(F)
- SOR/89-395, s. 2(F)

:::

### [Prescribed Form of Inspection Certificate]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581651 .Subheading}

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** An inspection certificate
referred to in section 32 of the Act shall be in the form set out

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of grain inspected on discharge from
  terminal elevators, in the document entitled Certificate Final for
  Grain, published by the Commission, as amended from time to time; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of all other grain in the document
  entitled Certificate for Grain, published by the Commission, as
  amended from time to time.

### [Grain Appeal Procedures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581658 .Subheading}

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** An application for the
reinspection of grain under section 39 of the Act shall be made in
writing and include the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the identification of the parcel of grain from which
  the official sample was taken;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and location of the elevator or other place
  where the official sample was taken;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date of the official inspection; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the grade and dockage assigned to the grain by the
  official inspection.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-813, s. 2
- SOR/89-376, ss. 12(F), 14(F)
- SOR/89-395, s. 2(F)
- SOR/93-197, s. 2
- SOR/96-508, s. 9
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2003-284, s. 8

- SOR/2013-111, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
section 40 of the Act, an appeal lies under section 39 of the Act in
respect of grain that has been officially inspected on discharge from a
primary elevator to a terminal elevator.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, s. 14(F)
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2001-273, s. 7
- SOR/2004-198, s. 7
- SOR/2013-111, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 12"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** The result of an appeal
to the chief grain inspector for Canada shall be given without delay in
writing by the chief grain inspector for Canada to the appellant and to
the operator of the elevator where the grain was officially inspected.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2001-273, s. 7
- SOR/2004-198, s. 7
- SOR/2013-111, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** If, as a result of an
appeal taken under section 39 of the Act to the chief grain inspector
for Canada, a grade is assigned to the grain that is different from the
grade previously assigned to it, the inspection certificate revised in
accordance with section 41 of the Act shall bear the date on which the
appeal was decided.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, s. 14(F)
- SOR/96-508, s. 10(E)
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2004-198, s. 7
- SOR/2013-111, s. 8
:::

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Licences and Licensees]{.HTitleText1} {#h-581675 .Part}

### [Exemptions from Licensing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581676 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2001-273, s. 8

\]
:::

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following types of elevators are exempted under paragraph 117(a) of
  the Act from the licensing requirements of the Act:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an elevator constructed for the purpose of handling
    and storing grain as part of the operation of a feed mill;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a process elevator, other than an elevator referred
    to in paragraph (a), if the operator of the elevator does not
    purchase grain from producers, does not assume obligations to
    producers for the payment of money in respect of grain and allows
    the Commission access to the operator's records for the elevator;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an elevator constructed for the purpose of handling
    and storing grain as part of the operation of a seed cleaning plant,
    if the operator of the seed cleaning plant does not operate the
    elevator to handle, store or purchase grain except grain for seed
    purposes and allows the Commission access to the operator's records
    for the elevator; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any elevator, if the operator of the elevator
    operates the elevator only as an agent or mandatary for licensees,
    the licensees provide security to the Commission for all grain
    received into the elevator and the operator allows the Commission
    access to the operator's records for the elevator.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The grain handling operation of a grain dealer is
  exempted under paragraph 117(a) of the Act from the licensing
  requirements of the Act if the grain dealer allows the Commission
  access to the grain dealer's records for dealing in and handling
  western grain and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the grain dealer does not deal in or handle western
    grain except as an agent or mandatary for licensees and the
    licensees provide security to the Commission for all western grain
    transactions handled by the grain dealer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the grain dealer does not deal in or handle western
    grain except grain for seed purposes; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the grain dealer does not purchase western grain
    from producers and does not assume obligations to producers for the
    payment of money in respect of western grain.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, s. 14(F)
- SOR/89-395, s. 1
- SOR/93-197, s. 3
- SOR/96-508, s. 11
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2001-273, s. 9
- SOR/2002-255, s. 4
- SOR/2004-198, s. 8
- SOR/2006-206, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[15.1 and 15.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15.1_and_15.2
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2000-213, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

### [General Conditions of Licensing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581692 .Subheading}

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** It is a condition of
every licence to operate an elevator that the licensee shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} store all grain received in an elevator building
  described in the application for the licence;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify the Commission in writing of any functional
  alteration to the elevator building described in the application or
  any functional alteration respecting grain flows or sampling or
  control mechanisms, and of any addition to any equipment associated
  with the elevator, within 15 days after plans for the alteration or
  addition become available;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} without delay notify the Commission in writing of any
  damage to, or destruction of, any elevator building described in the
  application, or damage to, or destruction or removal of, any equipment
  required by the Commission to be installed in the elevator building;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} without delay notify the Commission in writing of any
  damage to, or the destruction of, any grain stored in any elevator
  building described in the application;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} keep each elevator building and all associated
  equipment in good repair and in good working order;

- [(e.1)]{.lawlabel} keep sampling and weighing equipment and areas
  surrounding the equipment clean and accessible; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} keep the licence posted in a conspicuous place in the
  elevator.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, s. 14(F), 16(F)
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2001-273, s. 10
- SOR/2002-255, s. 5
- SOR/2003-284, s. 9(E)
- SOR/2004-198, s. 9
- SOR/2005-361, s. 4
:::

### [Security]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581703 .Subheading}

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** The period prescribed for
the purpose of paragraph 49(3)(a) of the Act is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if an elevator receipt or grain receipt is issued on
  delivery of the grain, 90 days; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a cash purchase ticket or other bill of exchange
  is issued on delivery of the grain or is later issued on surrender of
  an elevator receipt or grain receipt in respect of the grain, the
  lesser of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 90 days, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the period that ends 30 days after the day on
    which the cash purchase ticket or other bill of exchange is issued.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2005-361, s. 5
:::

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** Security is not required
from an applicant for a licence or from a licensee if the applicant or
licensee is an agent of Her Majesty in right of Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 2, 14(F), 16(F)
- SOR/96-508, s. 12
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
:::

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 49(5) of the Act, the prescribed percentage of security
realized or enforced is 100%.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 10(F), 14(F), 16(F)
- SOR/89-393, s. 3
- SOR/93-24, s. 1
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
:::

### [Records to Be Kept by Licensees]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581717 .Subheading}

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** Each licensee shall keep,
for at least six years, all documents that relate to grain that was
delivered to, stored by, shipped or otherwise disposed of by the
licensee and that specify whether the grain was purchased, received for
storage or received for sale on commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/85-677, s. 3
- SOR/89-376, s. 10(F), 11(F), 14(F), 16(F)

### [Obligation and Security Reports]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581732 .Subheading}

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** No later than the 15th
day of every month, each licensed grain dealer, primary elevator
operator and process elevator operator shall submit to the Commission,
on the appropriate form supplied by the Commission or in an electronic
format acceptable to it, a report respecting the licensee's outstanding
obligations for the payment of money or the delivery of grain to holders
of elevator receipts, grain receipts and cash purchase tickets and the
security amount available to meet those obligations at the end of the
preceding month.

### [Storage Charges if Licensee Unable to Deliver Grain]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581760 .Subheading}

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** With respect to a period
referred to in subsection 53(2) of the Act, the special maximum storage
charge at an elevator is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the period has lasted seven days, for the next
  seven days of the period, 75% of the storage charge that could
  otherwise be made by the operator of the elevator for that type of
  storage; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the period has lasted 14 days, for the remainder
  of the period, 50% of the storage charge that could otherwise be made
  by the operator of the elevator for that type of storage.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 14(F), 16(F)
- SOR/96-508, s. 18
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
:::

### [Public Notices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581766 .Subheading}

**[[29.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.1 .sectionLabel}** Each licensee who
operates an elevator shall keep posted in a conspicuous place in the
elevator the schedule of the current charges filed with the Commission
under subsection 50(1) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-198, s. 12
- [SOR/2024-43, s. 5](#1447998-1448415){.wb-lbx

## [PART 4.1]{.HLabel1}[Grain Delivery Contracts]{.HTitleText1} {#h-581770 .Part}

**[[29.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.2 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[delivery period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to a contract
for the purchase of grain, the period specified in the contract during
which grain is to be delivered by the producer to the licensee.
(*[période de livraison]{.otherLang lang="fr"}*)

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-191, s. 1
:::

- **[[29.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any contract between a producer and
  a licensee for the purchase of grain within a delivery period must
  include a provision stating that, in the event that delivery of the
  kind and grade of grain indicated in the contract is not accepted by
  the licensee during the delivery period, a penalty is to be paid by
  the licensee to the producer.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The contract must also include provisions stating
  that the penalty

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is agreed on by the producer and the licensee in
    the contract;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} applies to the remaining undelivered portion of
    grain that was contracted for but that was not accepted by the
    licensee during the delivery period; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is payable either at the time that the delivery of
    grain is completed by the producer or on another day otherwise
    agreed on by the producer and the licensee.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the penalty agreed on by the producer and the
  licensee is based on a daily amount, the penalty is payable for each
  day during the period beginning on the first day after the day on
  which the delivery period expires and ending on the day on which the
  total amount of grain specified in the contract is accepted and
  received by the licensee, or on another day otherwise agreed on by the
  producer and the licensee.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-191, s. 1
- SOR/2016-256, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29.3-20140801.html "Link to previous version of section 29.3"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[Elevators, Grain Dealers and Grain Handling]{.HTitleText1} {#h-581783 .Part}

### [Shrinkage Allowance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581784 .Subheading}

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** The maximum shrinkage
allowance that may be made on the delivery of grain to any licensed
elevator is zero.

::: HistoricalNote

- SOR/96-508, s. 19(E)

### [Receipt of Grain into a Licensed Primary Elevator]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581789 .Subheading}

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  cash purchase ticket or primary elevator receipt issued by the
  operator of a licensed primary elevator shall be set out in accordance
  with the document entitled Cash Purchase Ticket or Combined Primary
  Elevator Receipt, as appropriate, published by the Commission, as
  amended from time to time.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If grain is purchased by the operator of a licensed
  primary elevator, the operator shall issue a cash purchase ticket
  without delay after the grain is unloaded.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If grain is received for storage at a licensed
  primary elevator, the operator of the elevator shall issue a primary
  elevator receipt without delay after the grain is unloaded.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-55, s. 3
- SOR/84-626, s. 4
- SOR/85-678, s. 3
- SOR/89-376, ss. 12(F), 14(F), 20(F)

  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-33-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 33"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Sampling on Delivery]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581796 .Subheading}

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of sections 35 and 36, on the delivery of grain to a
  licensed primary elevator, a portion of at least 1 kg from a sample of
  the grain that is considered by the elevator operator and the person
  delivering the grain to be representative of the grain shall be taken
  from each load and retained either at the elevator or in accordance
  with any other instructions agreed to by the operator and the
  producer.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The sample shall be retained for the shortest of the
  following periods:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the period that ends seven days after the day on
    which the elevator operator issues the primary elevator receipt;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the period that ends when an agreement on grade and
    dockage is made between the elevator operator and the producer and
    an appropriate primary elevator receipt or cash purchase ticket has
    been issued; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the period that ends when the representative
    portion of the sample is forwarded in accordance with paragraph
    36(1)(d).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2002-255, s. 9
- SOR/2004-198, s. 14
- SOR/2008-314, s. 4
- [SOR/2022-195, s. 1](#1376050-1376345){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1376050-1376345.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-34-20081212.html "Link to previous version of section 34"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Dockage Determination on Receipt at Licensed Primary Elevator]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581800 .Subheading}

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of a licensed primary elevator shall make an accurate
  determination of dockage in grain delivered at the elevator by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} taking a representative portion of at least 1 kg
    from the sample referred to in section 34;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} testing the sample with equipment of a type
    authorized by the Commission and hand-picking, if necessary, any
    portion that is not separable by that equipment; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} computing the dockage to the nearest 0.1%.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the person delivering grain to a licensed primary
  elevator so requests, the test to determine the dockage in the grain
  shall be made in that person's presence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/84-626, s. 6
- SOR/89-376, ss. 14(F), 16(F)
- SOR/95-386, s. 5
- SOR/96-508, s. 23

- SOR/2002-255, s. 10

- SOR/2013-111, s. 10
:::

### [Final Quality Determination]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581810 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2024-43, s. 6

\]
:::

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  operator of a licensed primary elevator and the producer do not agree
  on the grade of the grain or the dockage in the grain delivered and an
  interim primary elevator receipt is issued, the operator shall take a
  representative portion of at least 1 kg from the sample referred to in
  section 34 and shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} put the representative portion in a container that
    is supplied by the operator or the person delivering the grain and
    that the operator and that person agree will maintain the integrity
    of the sample;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} identify the container with the name of the owner
    of the grain and with the interim primary elevator receipt number;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} mark the container "For Final Quality
    Determination"; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} forward the container to the nearest regional
    inspection office of the Commission, with all shipping charges
    prepaid, accompanied by a written request from the operator or the
    owner of the grain that an inspector examine the representative
    portion and report to the persons named in the request what grade
    and dockage the inspector would assign to the grain if the
    examination were an official inspection.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} After the applicable period referred to in 34(2)
  has expired in respect of a sample, the operator or the owner of the
  grain may not make a written request under paragraph (1)(d) in
  relation to the sample.

- [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the elevator shall take the
  representative portion in the presence of the person delivering the
  grain, if the producer so requires.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the representative portion, an
  inspector at the regional inspection office of the Commission shall
  examine it, determine its grade and dockage and transmit a copy of the
  determination to each person named in the request.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any person who has an interest in the grain and who
  is dissatisfied with the determination of the inspector may, within 15
  days after the date of the examination, request the chief grain
  inspector for Canada to re-examine the representative portion.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the representative portion, the chief
  grain inspector for Canada shall examine it, determine its grade and
  dockage and transmit a copy of the determination to each person named
  in the request.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The determination of the chief grain inspector for
  Canada is final.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the final determination, the operator
  of the elevator shall exchange the interim primary elevator receipt
  for the appropriate primary elevator receipt or cash purchase ticket
  indicating the grade and dockage assigned to the representative
  portion in accordance with this section.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, in this section, "grade"
  includes the tough, damp, moist and wet grades established in the [Off
  Grades of Grain and Grades of Screenings
  Order](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._890).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2002-255, s. 11
- SOR/2003-284, s. 15
- SOR/2004-198, s. 15
- SOR/2008-314, s. 6
- SOR/2013-111, s. 11

  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-43, s. 6](#1448000-1448420){.wb-lbx

### [Identity Preservation of special Bin Grain]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581826 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2004-198, s. 16(E)

\]
:::

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  grain is lawfully offered at a licensed primary elevator for special
  binning and the operator of the elevator agrees to specially bin the
  grain, the operator and person delivering the grain shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} put a sample of the grain in a container that is of
    a type acceptable to the Commission, that will maintain the
    integrity of the sample and that is supplied by the operator or the
    person delivering the grain;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} identify the container with the name of the owner
    of the grain and with the special bin primary elevator receipt
    number; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} mark the container "Special Bin".

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person delivering the grain to the elevator shall
  secure the container by means of a lock or seal.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator shall keep the container in a locked
  storage cabinet or storeroom at the elevator, for not less than 30
  days.

- [(4) to (7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2004-198, s. 17\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/84-626, s. 8
- SOR/89-376, ss. 14(F), 16(F)
- SOR/95-386, s. 7
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2002-255, s. 12
- SOR/2003-284, s. 16
- SOR/2004-198, s. 17
- SOR/2008-314, s. 7
:::

[Previous
Version](section-37-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 37"){.PITLink}

**[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2004-198, s. 18\]]{.Repealed}

### [Drying of Grain at Licensed Primary Elevators]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581840 .Subheading}

- **[[38.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to these Regulations, the
  operator of a licensed primary elevator may dry tough, damp, moist or
  wet grain at the elevator in accordance with orders of the Commission.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of a licensed primary elevator shall
  issue an elevator grain drying receipt in a form supplied by the
  Commission or accepted by the Commission for each load of grain
  containing excessive moisture delivered to the elevator for artificial
  drying.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder of the drying receipt may surrender the
  receipt to the operator and take from the operator any other
  appropriate receipts that the circumstances require or take delivery
  of the grain.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-255, s. 12
:::

### [Cleaning of Grain]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581847 .Subheading}

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, in
  accordance with section 63 of the Act, grain is lawfully offered for
  storage at a licensed primary elevator and a request is made that it
  be cleaned, the elevator receipt that is required to be issued under
  that section shall be a combined primary elevator receipt set out in
  accordance with the document entitled Combined Primary Elevator
  Receipt, published by the Commission, as amended from time to time,
  and endorsed as follows:

  "To be cleaned before being shipped or settled for."

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of the elevator receipt shall surrender it
  after the grain is cleaned, and the operator of the licensed primary
  elevator shall then issue the appropriate primary elevator receipt or
  cash purchase ticket for the grain, adjusted to the grade and quantity
  of the grain after cleaning.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If grain that is delivered for cleaning is returned
  to the owner of the grain, the operator of the licensed primary
  elevator shall issue a cleaning receipt for the grain, in a form
  acceptable to the Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-543, s. 1
- SOR/89-376, ss. 10(F), 14(F), 18(F)
- SOR/96-508, s. 26
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2003-284, s. 18
- SOR/2004-198, s. 19
- [SOR/2021-124, s. 7](#1298256-1312991){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1298256-1312991.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-39-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 39"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Shipment of Grain from a Licensed Primary Elevator]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581855 .Subheading}

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of a licensed primary elevator shall issue all shipping bills
  for grain shipped from the elevator and shall notify of each shipment
  any persons that the holder of a primary elevator receipt directs.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If grain is shipped from a licensed primary elevator,
  the operator of the elevator shall, on request from the holder of the
  primary elevator receipts in respect of the grain, give the holder
  particulars as to the grain shipped.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a conveyance is supplied at a licensed primary
  elevator on the application of any person other than the operator of
  the elevator, the operator shall not load into that conveyance any
  grain other than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of specially binned grain, grain
    offered by that person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of grain other than specially binned
    grain,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} grain offered by that person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} grain that is of the same kind, grade and
      quantity as the grain referred to in the elevator receipts
      surrendered by that person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 11(F), 12(F), 14(F), 15(F), 18(F)
- SOR/93-24, s. 4
- SOR/96-508, ss. 27, 61(F)
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2003-284, s. 19(F)
:::

**[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** The operator of a
licensed primary elevator

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall, on request by the Commission, produce elevator
  receipts or bills of lading for all grain that has been shipped from
  the elevator for which primary elevator receipts are outstanding; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall not assign, mortgage, pledge or hypothecate any
  grain stored in the elevator for which primary elevator receipts are
  outstanding.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 12(F), 14(F)
- SOR/94-508, s. 1
- SOR/95-336, s. 10(F)
- SOR/96-508, ss. 28, 61(F)
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
:::

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** If the operator of a
licensed primary elevator requires the holder of a primary elevator
receipt to take delivery of the grain in accordance with section 65 of
the Act, the operator shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} deliver to the holder of the primary elevator receipt
  in person, or send by registered mail to the holder's latest known
  address, a notice that

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} identifies the receipt issued in respect of the
    grain,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} makes the demand that the grain be removed from
    the elevator, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} shows the final date on which the holder may take
    delivery of the grain; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} forward to the Commission a duplicate copy of the
  notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 10(F), 12(F), 14(F), 18(F)
- SOR/89-395, s. 2(F)
- SOR/93-24, s. 5
- SOR/96-508, ss. 29, 61(F)
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
:::

### [Elevator Receipts --- Right of Redelivery]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581879 .Subheading}

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2003-284, s.
  20\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a primary elevator receipt who waives
  the right to demand delivery from the operator of the elevator of the
  grain referred to in the receipt shall sign the following waiver set
  out on the receipt:

  "I hereby waive the right to demand delivery of the grain described in
  this receipt from the operator of the elevator."

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 11(F), 14(F), 15(F), 18(F)
- SOR/89-395, s. 2(F)
- SOR/93-24, s. 6
- SOR/96-508, s. 30
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2003-284, s. 20
:::

### [Receipt of Grain into a Licensed Process Elevator]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581886 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2001-273, s. 15

\]
:::

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** A grain receipt or a cash
purchase ticket that is required by subsection 78(2) of the Act to be
issued by the operator of a licensed process elevator on the receipt of
grain from a producer into the elevator shall be issued on delivery of
the grain by the producer and shall be set out in accordance with the
document entitled Grain Receipt or Cash Purchase Ticket, as appropriate,
published by the Commission, as amended from time to time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, s. 14(F)
- SOR/96-508, s. 31
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2003-284, s. 21

### [Receipt of Grain by a Licensed Grain Dealer]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581892 .Subheading}

- **[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of this section, delivery of grain to an agent or
  mandatary of a licensed grain dealer is considered to be delivery to
  the licensed grain dealer.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A grain receipt or a cash purchase ticket that is
  required by subsection 81(1) of the Act to be issued by a licensed
  grain dealer shall be issued on receipt of western grain delivered by
  a producer or on being entitled to western grain delivered to an
  elevator by a producer, and shall be set out in accordance with the
  document entitled Grain Receipt or Cash Purchase Ticket, as
  appropriate, published by the Commission, as amended from time to
  time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2001-273, s. 16
- SOR/2002-255, s. 13
- SOR/2003-284, s. 22
- SOR/2006-206, s. 4

:::

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2003-284, s. 23\]]{.Repealed}

### [Direct Hit Shipments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581899 .Subheading}

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** The operator of a
licensed terminal elevator may receive grain without causing it to be
inspected and weighed and without issuing an elevator receipt for it if
the grain is transferred directly from a railway car or other conveyance
to a ship and is officially inspected and weighed on transfer to the
ship.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 10(F), 11(F), 14(F), 18(F), 20(F)
- SOR/93-24, s. 7
- SOR/96-508, s. 33
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2002-255, s. 14
- SOR/2004-198, s. 20
- SOR/2005-361, s. 7
- SOR/2013-111, s. 12
:::

[Previous
Version](section-47-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 47"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Receipt and Discharge of Grain from Licensed Terminal Elevators]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581903 .Subheading}

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** The operator of a
licensed terminal elevator may receive grain without causing it to be
inspected or weighed if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is eastern grain that was not delivered to the
  elevator by or on behalf of a producer;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is imported grain; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is western grain that has been previously
  inspected and weighed at a licensed terminal elevator.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-55, s. 1
- SOR/2013-111, s. 12
- [SOR/2020-63, s. 2](#1243525-1254012){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1243525-1254012.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-43, s. 7](#1448005-1448422){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1448005-1448422.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-48-20200701.html "Link to previous version of section 48"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** The operator of a
licensed terminal elevator may discharge grain without causing it to be
officially inspected and officially weighed if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is for export to the United States; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is not for export.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-111, s. 12
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-49-20100311.html "Link to previous version of section 49"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Dockage Determination at Licensed Terminal Elevator]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581915 .Subheading}

**[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** The operator of a
licensed terminal elevator shall make an accurate determination of
dockage of grain delivered at the elevator, computing the dockage to the
nearest 0.1%.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 14(F), 18(F)
- SOR/96-508, s. 37
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2002-255, s. 15

- SOR/2013-111, s. 12
:::

Version](section-50-20100311.html "Link to previous version of section 50"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Reinspection by the Chief Grain Inspector for Canada]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581919 .Subheading}

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application and a sample referred to in subsection 70(5) of the Act,
  for reinspection by the chief grain inspector for Canada, shall be
  forwarded to the chief grain inspector for Canada within five days
  after the date of the original inspection.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application referred to in subsection (1) shall
  include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the identification of the parcel of grain from
    which the sample was taken;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and location of the licensed terminal
    elevator where the sample was taken;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date of the original inspection; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the grade and dockage assigned to the parcel of
    grain.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A sample of grain referred to in subsection (1) shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consist of at least 1 kg;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be taken in such a manner as to ensure that it is
    an average and representative sample of the parcel of grain from
    which it is taken;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be forwarded, with all shipping charges prepaid, in
    a container that will maintain the integrity of the sample;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} be accompanied by a form acceptable to the
    Commission that states the name and post office address of each
    person to whom the report respecting the grade and dockage of the
    sample is to be sent; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} be identified on the form by a distinguishing
    number or mark that the shipper has not previously used in respect
    of any other sample during that crop year.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-393, s. 4
- SOR/96-508, s. 38
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2004-198, s. 22
- SOR/2013-111, s. 12
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-51-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 51"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Sampling of Grain if not Inspected on Receipt at a Licensed Terminal Elevator]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581935 .Subheading}

**[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** A sample referred to in
subsection 70.1(2) of the Act shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be taken on loading of the grain into a railcar;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} consist of at least 1 kg;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} be taken in such a manner as to ensure that it is an
  average and representative sample of the parcel of grain from which it
  is taken; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} be forwarded to the Commission, with all shipping
  charges prepaid, in a container that will maintain the integrity of
  the sample.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-111, s. 12
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-52-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 52"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Sampling of Grain on Receipt from a Licensed Terminal Elevator where Grain is not Inspected]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581943 .Subheading}

**[[52.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52.1 .sectionLabel}** A sample referred to
in subsection 70.4(2) of the Act shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} consist of at least 1 kg;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} be taken in such a manner as to ensure that it is an
  average and representative sample of the parcel of grain from which it
  is taken; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} be forwarded to the Commission, with all shipping
  charges prepaid, in a container that will maintain the integrity of
  the sample.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-111, s. 12
:::

### [Discharge of Grain Containing Dockage]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581950 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2005-361, s. 9

\]
:::

**[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** If the operator of a
licensed terminal elevator wishes to obtain the permission of the
Commission under paragraph 75(b) of the Act to discharge from the
elevator grain containing dockage, the operator shall make a written
request to the Commission stating

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the kind, grade and quantity of the grain;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the final destination of the grain; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the reasons for the request.

### [Special Binning]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581962 .Subheading}

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** The operator of a
licensed terminal elevator may specially bin any grain if either

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the following conditions are met, namely,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the operator and the person wishing to specially
    bin the grain enter into a special bin agreement set out in
    accordance with the document entitled Special Bin Agreement,
    published by the Commission, as amended from time to time,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all the grain is binned in accordance with the
    agreement,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} not more than 30% of the total operating capacity
    of the elevator is contractually committed to special binning of
    grain, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the agreement is filed by the operator
    with the Commission before the commencement date of the agreement;
    or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the grain is binned according to protein level.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/84-626, s. 11
- SOR/89-376, s. 14(F)
- SOR/96-508, s. 39
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2003-284, s. 26

- SOR/2013-111, s. 14

  role="button"}
:::

### [Terminal Elevator Receipts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581973 .Subheading}

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** A terminal elevator
receipt shall be set out in accordance with the document entitled
Terminal Elevator Receipt, published by the Commission, as amended from
time to time.

### [Compulsory Removal of Grain from Licensed Terminal Elevators]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581977 .Subheading}

- **[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  notice in writing given by the operator of a licensed terminal
  elevator to the last known holder of a terminal elevator receipt under
  section 77 of the Act shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be delivered to the holder of the receipt in person
    or sent by registered mail to the holder's latest known address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} show the numbers of the elevator receipts issued in
    respect of the grain to be removed from the elevator;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} make the demand that the grain be removed from the
    elevator; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} show the final date by which the holder is required
    to take delivery of the grain.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the operator of a licensed terminal elevator gives
  the notice in accordance with subsection (1), the operator shall at
  the same time forward a copy of the notice to the Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-376, ss. 12(F), 14(F)
- SOR/89-395, s. 2(F)
- SOR/96-508, s. 40
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2

:::

### [Hazardous substances and Contaminated Grain in Elevators]{.HTitleText2} {#h-581988 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2004-198, s. 29

\]
:::

- **[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  hazardous substances, other than products used for fumigating and
  spraying infested grain, shall be used for the treatment of grain at
  an elevator.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No hazardous substances shall be stored in an area of
  a licensed elevator or annex to it unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there is no direct access from the area to any area
    used for the handling or storage of grain;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator of the elevator has specifically
    designated the area for the storage of hazardous substances; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the storage of hazardous substances in the area is
    not a danger to the handling or storage of grain at the elevator.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If grain that is being received into, stored in or
  discharged from a licensed elevator is found to be contaminated, the
  operator of the elevator shall without delay notify the Commission and
  shall dispose of the contaminated grain in accordance with directions
  given by the chief grain inspector for Canada under an order of the
  Commission under paragraph 118(d) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2001-273, s. 20
:::

**[[62 and 63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62_and_63
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2001-273, s. 21\]]{.Repealed}

### [Infestation in Elevators]{.HTitleText2} {#h-582001 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2001-273, s. 22

\]
:::

**[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** If grain stored in an
elevator is found to be infested, the operator of the elevator shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the absence of Commission personnel at the
  elevator, without delay provide the Commission with full particulars
  of the nature and extent of the infestation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} send to the Commission in a sealed container a 1 kg
  sample of the grain containing specimens of the pests with which the
  grain is infested;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} treat the infested grain in accordance with
  instructions issued by the Commission; and

- [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2005-361, s. 11\]]{.Repealed}

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} clean out and treat any emptied annex or bin that
  contained infested grain and any equipment used in handling that grain
  in accordance with instructions issued by the Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-813, s. 6
- SOR/89-376, s. 14(F)
- SOR/89-394, s. 2
- SOR/89-395, s. 2(F)
- SOR/96-508, s. 42
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2001-273, s. 23
- SOR/2004-198, s. 30
- SOR/2005-361, s. 11
:::

### [Declaration Respecting Grain]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1254017 .Subheading}

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  declaration respecting grain referred to in section 83.1 of the Act is
  set out in the document entitled Declaration of Eligibility for
  Delivery of Grain, published by the Commission, as amended from time
  to time.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The declaration must be made and provided for every
  kind of grain --- and for each class, if any, of every kind of grain
  --- that is set out in the document entitled Kinds of Grain that
  Require a Declaration of Eligibility for Delivery of Grain, published
  by the Commission, as amended from time to time.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The declaration must be provided to the person who
  receives the grain delivery.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The declaration must be made and provided at least
  once for every crop year, no later than the date of the first delivery
  of grain to which the declaration applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-63, s. 3](#1243527-1254024){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1243527-1254024.html"
  role="button"}

  role="button"}

  role="button"}
:::

## [PART 6]{.HLabel1}[Carriage of Grain]{.HTitleText1} {#h-582015 .Part}

### [Producer Railway Cars]{.HTitleText2} {#h-582016 .Subheading}

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} One or
  more producers of grain who wish to apply for a railway car under
  section 87 of the Act shall send to the Commission a completed
  application in the document entitled Application for Producer to
  Obtain Railway Cars, published by the Commission, as amended from time
  to time.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a railway car that is allocated by the Commission
  as a result of the application arrives at a delivery point chosen by
  the producer or producers, as the case may be, the producer or
  producers shall load the grain declared in the application form
  directly from a siding or private bulk-loading facility into the
  railway car.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/84-626, s. 14
- SOR/89-376, ss. 14(F), 18(F)
- SOR/96-508, s. 45
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2003-284, s. 29
- [SOR/2021-124, s. 13](#1298268-1313003){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1298268-1313003.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-68-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 68"){.PITLink}

**[[68.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68.1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2000-213, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

### [Condition of Conveyances]{.HTitleText2} {#h-582023 .Subheading}

**[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** A conveyance is not in
fit condition to receive grain unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is clean, dry, structurally sound and free from
  infestation; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the conveyance is a ship, the holds are
  sufficiently protected while loading during rainstorms to prevent
  excessive water from mixing with the grain.

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2004-198, s. 32\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/84-626, s. 15
- SOR/89-376, ss. 12(F), 14(F), 17(F), 18(F), 20(F)
- SOR/89-393, s. 6
- SOR/89-395, s. 2(F)
- SOR/93-25, s. 6
- SOR/96-508, s. 47
- SOR/2000-213, s. 2
- SOR/2002-255, s. 19
- SOR/2004-198, s. 32
- SOR/2005-361, s. 12
:::

### [Transport of Grain]{.HTitleText2} {#h-582030 .Subheading}

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person may transport or cause to be transported

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2020-63, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any grain for export to a final destination in the
    United States on condition that the Commission is presented, in an
    electronic format acceptable to the Commission, with a report
    respecting any grain exports that took place during the preceding
    week; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any grain for export by container on the condition
    that the Commission is presented, in an electronic format acceptable
    to the Commission, with a report respecting any grain exports that
    took place during the preceding week.

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2004-198, s. 33\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2004-198, s. 33\]]{.Repealed}

#### [Notes:]{.HTitleText2} {#notes .SchedHeadL2}

##### [Fees for all items]{.HTitleText3} {#fees-for-all-items .SchedHeadL3}

- [1]{.lawlabel} Fees are exclusive of the goods and services tax.

##### [Fees for items 1 and 4]{.HTitleText3} {#fees-for-items-1-and-4 .SchedHeadL3}

- [2]{.lawlabel} Fees are calculated to the nearest cent.

##### [Fees for items 2 and 5]{.HTitleText3} {#fees-for-items-2-and-5 .SchedHeadL3}

- [3]{.lawlabel} If more than one certificate is required for a railway
  car, truck or container, separate inspection and weighing fees will be
  applied for each certificate issued (per item 14).

::::::::: section
:::::::: {#582065 .Schedule}
::: {}
## [SCHEDULE 2]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 3, 4 and 4.1)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-582066 .scheduleLabel}
:::

::: {}
## [FORM 1]{.scheduleLabel}[Oath or Solemn Affirmation of Office of Commissioner]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#form-1oath-or-solemn-affirmation-of-office-of-commissioner .scheduleLabel}
:::

I do solemnly swear (or affirm) that I will faithfully, truly and
impartially, to the best of my judgment, skill and ability, execute and
perform the office of commissioner of the Canadian Grain Commission and
that, while I continue to hold that office, I will not either directly
or indirectly, as owner, shareholder, director, officer, partner or
otherwise, be engaged in any commercial dealings in grain or the
carriage of grain or have any pecuniary or proprietary interest in grain
or the carriage of grain, other than as a producer of grain. (*Add, in
the case where an oath is taken*, "So Help Me God.")

::: {}
## [FORM 2]{.scheduleLabel}[Oath or Solemn Affirmation of Office of Member of Grain Standards Committee]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#form-2oath-or-solemn-affirmation-of-office-of-member-of-grain-standards-committee .scheduleLabel}
:::

I do solemnly swear (or affirm) that I will faithfully and impartially
perform my duties as a member of the (Western) (Eastern) grain standards
committee to the best of my skill and ability. (*Add, in the case where
an oath is taken*, "So Help Me God.")

::: {}
## [FORM 3]{.scheduleLabel} {#form-3 .scheduleLabel}
:::

[\[Repealed, SOR/2013-111, s. 17\]]{.Repealed}

:::::: section

::: {}
## [SCHEDULE 3]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subsection 5(2))]{.OriginatingRef}[Grades of Grain]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-582079 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding

  **1** For the purpose of determining the characteristics of a grain,
  any value that is calculated in relation to a criterion set out in
  this schedule shall be:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the criterion is expressed as a whole number,
    rounded to the nearest whole number or, if the value is equidistant
    between two whole numbers, rounded up to the nearest whole number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the criterion is expressed as a decimal in
    tenths of a whole number, rounded to the nearest tenth of a whole
    number, or if the value is equidistant between two consecutive
    tenths, rounded up to the nearest tenth of a whole number; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the criterion is expressed as a decimal in
    hundredths of a whole number, rounded to the nearest hundredth of a
    whole number, or if the value is equidistant between two consecutive
    hundredths, rounded up to the nearest hundredth of a whole number.
